### Q1. JEE Main 2026 (08 April Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Consider the following reaction.
<img style="width: 400px" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q67_d1.jpg" />
The major product (P) formed is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img style="width: 250px" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q67_d2.jpg" />
- **(B)** <img style="width: 250px" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q67_d3.jpg" />
- **(C)** <img style="width: 250px" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q67_d4.jpg" />
- **(D)** <img style="width: 250px" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q67_d5.jpg" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <img style="width: 250px" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q67_d4.jpg" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

The reaction sequence proceeds as follows:

The reagent $\text{NaBH}_4$ is a mild reducing agent that selectively reduces the aldehyde group ($-\text{CHO}$) to a primary alcohol ($-\text{CH}_2\text{OH}$). The lactam (cyclic amide) ring remains unaffected.

Heating with aqueous $\text{NaOH}$ causes the base-catalyzed hydrolysis of the lactam ring. The amide bond breaks, forming a carboxylate salt ($-\text{COO}^-$) and a secondary amine ($-\text{NH}-$).

Acidification with $\text{H}_3\text{O}^+$ protonates the carboxylate ion to form a carboxylic acid ($-\text{COOH}$).

The final product is an open-chain amino acid derivative with the structure $\text{HOOC}-\text{CH}_2-\text{CH}_2-\text{CH}_2-\text{CH}_2-\text{NH}-\text{CH(CH}_3)-\text{CH}_2\text{OH}$. This matches the structure given in option (C).

Answer: <img style="width: 300px" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q67_d4.jpg" />

</details>

### Q2. JEE Main 2026 (06 April Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

'$x$' is the product which is obtained by the hydrolysis of prop-1-yne in the presence of mercuric sulphate under dilute acidic medium at $333$ K. '$y$' is the product which is obtained by the reaction of ethane nitrile with methyl magnesium bromide in dry ether followed by hydrolysis. IUPAC name of product obtained from '$x$' and '$y$' in the presence of barium hydroxide followed by heating is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** 2-Methylpent-4-en-3-one
- **(B)** 4-Methylpent-3-en-2-one
- **(C)** 4-Methylpent-1-ene
- **(D)** 2-Methylpent-3-one

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) 4-Methylpent-3-en-2-one**

**Detailed Solution:**

Hydrolysis of prop-1-yne in the presence of $HgSO_4$ and dilute $H_2SO_4$ yields acetone.

$CH_3-C \equiv CH + H_2O \xrightarrow{Hg^{2+}, H^+} CH_3-CO-CH_3$

Thus, $x$ is acetone.

Reaction of ethane nitrile ($CH_3CN$) with methyl magnesium bromide ($CH_3MgBr$) followed by hydrolysis also yields acetone.

$CH_3-C \equiv N + CH_3MgBr \rightarrow CH_3-C(CH_3)=NMgBr \xrightarrow{H_3O^+} CH_3-CO-CH_3$

Thus, $y$ is acetone.

Reaction of $x$ and $y$ (both acetone) in the presence of $Ba(OH)_2$ followed by heating results in an aldol condensation.

$2 CH_3-CO-CH_3 \xrightarrow{Ba(OH)_2} CH_3-C(OH)(CH_3)-CH_2-CO-CH_3$

Heating causes dehydration to form an unsaturated ketone.

$CH_3-C(OH)(CH_3)-CH_2-CO-CH_3 \xrightarrow{\Delta} CH_3-C(CH_3)=CH-CO-CH_3$

Numbering the principal carbon chain to give the lowest locant to the ketone group, the IUPAC name of the product is 4-methylpent-3-en-2-one.

Answer: 4-Methylpent-3-en-2-one

</details>

### Q3. JEE Main 2026 (05 April Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Given below are two statements:

Statement-I: The condensation reaction between $CH_3-CH=O$ and <img width=180px src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q66_d1.png"> under optimum pH will produce <img width=300px src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q66_d2.png">.

Statement-II: The molecule <img width=270px src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main_2026_april_watermarked/08APRS2__q66_d3.png"> will generate $Ph-CH=O$ in the presence of dilute acid.

In the light of the above statements, choose the correct answer from the options given below:

**Options:**

- **(A)** Both Statement I and Statement II are true
- **(B)** Both Statement I and Statement II are false
- **(C)** Statement I is true but Statement II is false
- **(D)** Statement I is false but Statement II is true

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) Statement I is false but Statement II is true**

**Detailed Solution:**

In Statement-I, evaluating the structure of semicarbazide ($\text{H}_2\text{N}-\text{NH}-\text{C}(=\text{O})-\text{NH}_2$), the lone pair of electrons on the $-\text{NH}_2$ group directly attached to the carbonyl carbon is delocalized due to resonance. The terminal $-\text{NH}_2$ group of the hydrazine part is not involved in resonance with the carbonyl group, making it significantly more nucleophilic.

Therefore, the condensation reaction of acetaldehyde with semicarbazide under optimum pH occurs at the terminal $-\text{NH}_2$ group to yield acetaldehyde semicarbazone:

$\text{CH}_3\text{CH}=\text{O} + \text{H}_2\text{N}-\text{NH}-\text{C}(=\text{O})-\text{NH}_2 \rightarrow \text{CH}_3\text{CH}=\text{N}-\text{NH}-\text{C}(=\text{O})-\text{NH}_2 + \text{H}_2\text{O}$

The product provided in Statement-I indicates an attack by the less nucleophilic amide nitrogen to form $\text{CH}_3\text{CH}=\text{N}-\text{C}(=\text{O})-\text{NH}-\text{NH}_2$, which is incorrect. Thus, Statement-I is false.

In Statement-II, the given molecule $\text{Ph}-\text{CH}(\text{OH})(\text{OCH}_3)$ is a hemiacetal. Hemiacetals are unstable in the presence of dilute aqueous acid and undergo rapid hydrolysis to regenerate the corresponding carbonyl compound and alcohol.

$\text{Ph}-\text{CH}(\text{OH})(\text{OCH}_3) \xrightarrow{\text{dil. H}^+} \text{Ph}-\text{CH}=\text{O} + \text{CH}_3\text{OH}$

This hydrolysis reaction successfully generates benzaldehyde ($\text{Ph}-\text{CH}=\text{O}$). Thus, Statement-II is true.

We conclude that Statement-I is false but Statement-II is true.

</details>

### Q4. JEE Main 2026 (02 April Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

An organic compound "$x$" where molar ratio of C, O and H are equal, on treatment with $50\%$ KOH under reflux followed by acidification produced "$y$". The most likely structure of "$y$" is:
[Molar mass of '$x$' is $58$ g mol$^{-1}$]

**Options:**

- **(A)** $CH_2 = CH - \overset{O}{\underset{\|}{C}} - OH$
- **(B)** $CH_3 - CH = CH - CH = O$
- **(C)** $O = C - OH, \, CH_2 - OH$ (with C-C bond)
- **(D)** $CH_3 - \overset{O}{\underset{\|}{C}} - OH$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) $O = C - OH, \, CH_2 - OH$ (with C-C bond)**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given the molar ratio of C, O, and H in compound $x$ is equal, the empirical formula is $CHO$.

Empirical mass $= 12 + 1 + 16 = 29$ g mol$^{-1}$.

Since the molar mass of $x$ is $58$ g mol$^{-1}$, the molecular formula is $(CHO)_2$ or $C_2H_2O_2$.

The structure of $x$ is glyoxal, $OHC-CHO$.

Treatment of glyoxal with $50\%$ KOH under reflux results in an intramolecular Cannizzaro reaction. One aldehyde group is oxidized to a carboxylate ion, and the other is reduced to a primary alcohol.

$OHC-CHO \xrightarrow{50\% \text{ KOH}} HOCH_2-COO^{-}K^{+}$

Subsequent acidification yields glycolic acid ($y$).

$HOCH_2-COO^{-}K^{+} \xrightarrow{H^{+}} HOCH_2-COOH$

The structure of $y$ is $HO-CH_2-COOH$.

Answer: $O = C - OH, \, CH_2 - OH$ (with C-C bond)

</details>

### Q5. JEE Main 2026 (24 January Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Given below are two statements: 
 <b>Statement I</b>: Cross aldol condensation between two different aldehydes will always produce four different products. 
 <b>Statement II</b>: When semicarbazide reacts with a mixture of benzaldehyde and acetophenone under optimum pH, it forms a condensation product with acetophenone only. 
 In the light of the above statements, choose the correct answer from the options given below

**Options:**

- **(A)** Statement I is false but Statement II is true
- **(B)** Both Statement I and Statement II are false
- **(C)** Both Statement I and Statement II are true
- **(D)** Statement I is true but Statement II is false

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) Both Statement I and Statement II are false**

**Detailed Solution:**

Statement I: Cross aldol condensation between two different aldehydes produces four products only if both aldehydes have $\alpha$-hydrogen atoms. If one aldehyde lacks $\alpha$-hydrogen (e.g., benzaldehyde and acetaldehyde), only two products are typically formed (one self and one cross). If neither has $\alpha$-hydrogen, no aldol condensation occurs. Thus, it does not 'always' produce four products. Statement I is false.

Statement II: Benzaldehyde is an aldehyde and acetophenone is a ketone. Aldehydes are generally more reactive than ketones towards nucleophilic addition reactions due to less steric hindrance and greater electronic deficiency of the carbonyl carbon. Therefore, semicarbazide will react faster with benzaldehyde than with acetophenone. The statement claiming it forms a product with acetophenone only is incorrect. Statement II is false.

</details>

### Q6. JEE Main 2026 (23 January Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

' $x$ ' is the product which is obtained from propanenitrile and stannous chloride in the presence of hydrochloric acid followed by hydrolysis. ' $y$ ' is the product which is obtained from the but-2-ene by the ozonolysis followed by hydrolysis. From the following, which product is not obtained when one mole of ' $x$ ' and one mole of ' $y$ ' react with each other in the presence of alkali followed by heating?

**Options:**

- **(A)** 2-Methylpent-2-enal
- **(B)** Pent-2-enal
- **(C)** 2-Methylbut-2-enal
- **(D)** 3-Methylbut-2-enal

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 3-Methylbut-2-enal**

**Detailed Solution:**

Product $x$ is obtained by the Stephen reduction of propanenitrile ($CH_3CH_2CN$).
$CH_3CH_2CN \xrightarrow{SnCl_2/HCl} CH_3CH_2CH=NH \xrightarrow{H_2O} CH_3CH_2CHO$ (Propanal).
Product $y$ is obtained by the reductive ozonolysis of but-2-ene ($CH_3CH=CHCH_3$).
$CH_3CH=CHCH_3 \xrightarrow{O_3, H_2O/Zn} 2CH_3CHO$ (Ethanal).
When propanal ($x$) and ethanal ($y$) react in the presence of alkali followed by heating, they undergo cross-aldol condensation.
Possible products are:
1. Self-aldol of propanal: $CH_3CH_2CHO + CH_3CH_2CHO \rightarrow CH_3CH_2CH=C(CH_3)CHO$ (2-Methylpent-2-enal).
2. Self-aldol of ethanal: $CH_3CHO + CH_3CHO \rightarrow CH_3CH=CHCHO$ (But-2-enal).
3. Cross-aldol (Ethanal as nucleophile): $CH_3CH_2CHO + CH_3CHO \rightarrow CH_3CH_2CH=CHCHO$ (Pent-2-enal).
4. Cross-aldol (Propanal as nucleophile): $CH_3CHO + CH_3CH_2CHO \rightarrow CH_3CH=C(CH_3)CHO$ (2-Methylbut-2-enal).
Comparing with the options, 3-Methylbut-2-enal is not formed in any of these reactions.

</details>

**Options:**

- **(A)** Both Statement I and Statement II are false
- **(B)** Statement I is true but Statement II is false
- **(C)** Statement I is false but Statement II is true
- **(D)** Both Statement I and Statement II are true

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) Both Statement I and Statement II are true**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\mathrm{k}_{\mathrm{eq}}=2280$ is for HCHO
$\mathrm{k}_{\text {eq }}=2000$ is for chloral
Both data is given in clayden and warren book. $\mathrm{k}_{\mathrm{eq}}>1$ because HCHO and chloral are more electrophilic.

</details>

### Q23. JEE Main 2024 (01 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>Match List – I with List –II.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/1bac9a95-2915-4ee5-8526-19c677805677.png" style="width: 759px; height: 324px;"/></p><p>Choose the correct answer from options given below:</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>III</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>IV</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>IV</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>III</mi></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>IV</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>III</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>III</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>IV</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>IV</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>II</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced><mi>III</mi></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><b>DIBAL</b>-<b>H</b><b> </b>i.e., Di isobutyl aluminium hydride causes partial reduction of cyanide group along with the ester group and further hydrolysis forms corresponding aldehyde as the product. The reaction is as follows,</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mfenced><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>5</mn></msub><msub><mi>COOC</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><mover><mo>→</mo><mrow><mtext> DIBAL- </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>,</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></mover><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mfenced><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>5</mn></msub><mi>CHO</mi></math></p><p>The Clemmensen reduction is a reaction that is used to reduce aldehydes or ketones to alkanes using hydrochloric acid and zinc amalgam.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><msub><mi>COC</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><mover><mo>→</mo><mrow><mi>Zn</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>Hg</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&amp;</mo><mtext> conc. </mtext><mi>HCl</mi></mrow></mover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub></math></p><p>A Grignard reagent is an organomagnesium compound which can be described by the <em>chemical formula ‘R-Mg-X’</em><em> </em>. Here aldehyde will react with Grignard's reagent to give alcohol.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><mi>CHO</mi><munderover><mo>→</mo><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>MgBr</mi></mrow></munderover><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><mi>CH</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>OH</mi><mo>)</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi>COCH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>COOC</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><mover><mo>→</mo><mrow><msub><mi>NaBH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow></mover><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>CH</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>OH</mi><mo>)</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>COOC</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub></math></p><p>In this reaction, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NaBH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></math> is used as a reducing agent, to get <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>CH</mi><mo>(</mo><mi>OH</mi><mo>)</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>COOC</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub></math> as a product.</p><p>Hence option B is the answer.</p>

</details>

### Q24. JEE Main 2024 (30 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>m-chlorobenzaldehyde on treatment with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>50</mn><mo>%</mo></math><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>KOH</mi></math> solution yields</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/247cf632-875e-4c2c-ba49-a3c9cbd6608e.png" style="width: 315px; height: 185px;"/>
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/ebf5247a-0d4f-4c84-9b7f-70eb808c531f.png" style="width: 401px; height: 180px;"/>
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/be766da0-d965-496e-a925-6e58e5db6ddf.png" style="width: 351px; height: 158px;"/>
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/12114879-7dfb-44a0-a97e-210fd170e723.png" style="width: 400px; height: 182px;"/>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/12114879-7dfb-44a0-a97e-210fd170e723.png" style="width: 400px; height: 182px;"/>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Meta–chlorobenzaldehyde will undergo Cannizzaro reaction with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>50</mn><mo>%</mo><mo> </mo><mi>KOH</mi></math> to give mchlorobenzoate ion and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>-</mo></math>chlorobenzyl alcohol. </p><p>Since <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>-</mo></math>chlorobenzaldehyde does not contain α hydrogen thus with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>50</mn><mo>%</mo><mo> </mo><mi>KOH</mi></math> it undergoes the Cannizzaro reaction. The Cannizzaro reaction for the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>-</mo></math>chlorobenzaldehyde is given as:</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/50073db2-f5e4-463e-adf9-af7f4a4d5472.png" style="width: 600px; height: 156px;"/>Since it is a disproportionation reaction so one molecule of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>-</mo></math>chlorobenzaldehyde is reduced to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>-</mo></math>chlorobenzyl alcohol at the cost of others which is oxidized potassium <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>-</mo></math>chlorobenzoate.</p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q25. JEE Main 2024 (30 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The compound formed by the reaction of ethanal with semicarbazide contains _______ number of nitrogen atoms.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **03**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><b>Semicarbazide</b> is the chemical compound with the formula <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>OC</mi><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math>. It is a water-soluble white solid. It is a derivative of urea.</p><p>Acetaldehyde reacts with semicarbazide give acetaldehyde semicarbazone, the reaction will be</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/12db933e-e21c-4366-978c-cb26003abebb.png" style="width: 500px; height: 302px;"/></p><p>Hence the product formed has three nitrogen atoms in it.</p>

</details>

<p>Identify the reagents used for the following conversion</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d49d7376-0aab-4890-a255-980c38d04d0f.png" style="width: 700px; height: 221px;"/></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>LiAlH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>NaOH</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>aq</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>/</mo><mi>KOH</mi><mo> </mo><mi>ethylene</mi><mo> </mo><mi>glycol</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>LiAlH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mo> </mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>NaOH</mi><mtext>(alc) </mtext></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>Zn</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>HCl</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>DIBAL</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>NaOH</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>aq</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>/</mo><mi>KOH</mi><mo> </mo><mo> </mo><mi>ethylene</mi><mo> </mo><mi>glycol</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>DIBAL</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>NaOH</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>alc</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>Zn</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>HCl</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>DIBAL</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><msub><mi>NaOH</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>alc</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow></msub><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>Zn</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>HCl</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Diisobutylaluminum hydride (DIBAL-H) is an organoaluminum reagent typically used for the reduction esters or nitriles to aldehydes. </p><p>In the second step intramolecular aldol condensation reaction takes place. Cyclization occurs if the α carbon atom and the second carbonyl carbon atom can bond to form a five- or six-membered ring.</p><p>The Clemmensen reduction is a reaction that is used to reduce aldehydes or ketones to alkanes using hydrochloric acid and zinc amalgam. In the same condition, alcohol converts to alkenes.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/3f811fdd-d017-402a-9d19-adbc42ba234d.png" style="width: 671px; height: 410px;"/></p>

</details>

### Q27. JEE Main 2023 (13 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mo>-</mo><mo>(</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>)</mo><mo>-</mo></math>Glyceraldehyde<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><munderover><mo>&#8594;</mo><munder><mrow><mi>ii</mi><mo>)</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>/</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow><mrow><mi>iii</mi><mo>)</mo><msub><mi>HNO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mrow></munder><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>)</mo><mi>HCN</mi></mrow></munderover></math></p><p>The products formed in the above reaction are</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <p>One optically active and one meso product</p>
- **(B)** Two optically inactive products
- **(C)** <p>Two optically active products</p>
- **(D)** <p>One optically inactive and one meso product</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <p>One optically active and one meso product</p>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The aldehyde group of glyceraldehyde reacts with hydrogen cyanide give cyanohydrin. Two isomers formed in the above step, because aldehyde is planar and changed to tetrahedral centre. On hydrolysis cyanide group convert to carboxylic acid. Upon reaction with nitric acid, the primary alcoholic group undergo oxidation to carboxylic acid group. One compound is meso compound, optically inactive and other compound is optically active.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/f48911b1-bde7-4a74-bd9e-58b0452ed2ff.png" style="width: 411px; height: 479px;" /> </p>

</details>

---

### Q28. JEE Main 2023 (12 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/95e1b3d2-6238-4a80-b8ae-95da86c91873.png" style="width: 581px; height: 180px;" /></p><p>The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> in compound &lsquo;<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math>&rsquo; is _____</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **15**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The reaction of aldehydes and ketones with ammonia forms imine derivatives, also known as Schiff bases (compounds having a C=N function). Imines can undergo nucleophilic addition reaction with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>KCN</mi></math>. Now, nitration takes place on benzene ring. Then amine group under acetylation reaction with acetic anhydride.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/a099decb-d24a-4dd8-9dab-24a62af96665.png" style="width: 687px; height: 317px;" /></p><p>Now, the carboxylic acid group undergo esterification reaction with ethyl alcohol in acidic medium. Now, the nitro groups present on benzene undergo reduction to amines with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>/</mo><mi>Pd</mi></math>. Now, the amino groups undergo diazotization with nitrous acid. Then substitution of iodine takes place at diazonium groups upon reaction with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>NaI</mi></math>.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/db064a8f-e8d6-4418-89ed-f4d83a449b3f.png" style="width: 888px; height: 388px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>15</mn></math></p>

</details>

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/a3cc058b-091c-4822-b803-381586f88bee.png" style="width: 400px; height: 106px;" /></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> in the above reaction is :</p>

**Detailed Solution:**

The reaction is shown below.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/339f8e83-8405-4d6a-a25c-16bfc14450dc.png" style="width: 500px; height: 260px;" /></p>

### Q30. JEE Main 2023 (11 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/35844d5b-7c55-49b0-91d3-364f3ac6328b-image.png" style="width: 500px; height: 103px;" /></p><p>The ratio x/y on completion of the above reaction is ______ .</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Acid-base reactions are faster than nucleophilic addition reaction. So, in the first step acid-base reaction takes place between alcoholic functional group and Grignard reagent. Now, the second mole of Grignard reagent to react with aldehyde functional group. Hence, at the end of the reaction a diol is produced on acidification.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/67f1d4b9-c85e-4a6e-817a-7bf45cecac15-image.png" style="width: 500px; height: 110px;" /></p><p> </p>

</details>

### Q31. JEE Main 2023 (11 Apr Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/f18f433e-b8e5-4061-a5eb-08999f766beb.png" style="width: 668px; height: 177px;" /></p><p>&lsquo;A&rsquo; and &lsquo;B&rsquo; in the above reactions are:</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/c7194914-d521-4fad-8c28-fabb7804eba0-image.png" style="width: 500px; height: 114px;" /></p>
- **(B)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/eadbee56-84ea-4ca3-b4d1-2fe7d2032a83-image.png" style="width: 500px; height: 82px;" /></p>
- **(C)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/3173c903-390f-4c68-ad5d-60f31520c119-image.png" style="width: 500px; height: 90px;" /></p>
- **(D)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d6326125-6f32-40ea-9456-2cdb7f7f160f-image.png" style="width: 500px; height: 109px;" /></p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/eadbee56-84ea-4ca3-b4d1-2fe7d2032a83-image.png" style="width: 500px; height: 82px;" /></p>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Oxidation of this substituted ketone will give a ketone and a carboxylic acid. This reaction takes place by the cleavage of carbon atoms near the carbonyl carbon and the nearby carbons. This cleavage can be from both the sides of carbonyl carbon thus producing two different carboxylic acids and ketones. The ketone formed converts to hydrocarbon in the presence hydrazine in alkaline medium. The overall reaction is shown below.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e2011e02-5da3-4548-8957-f5e107a9dacb-image.png" style="width: 500px; height: 372px;" /></p>

### Q33. JEE Main 2023 (31 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>Consider the following reaction</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Propanal</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>Methanal</mi><mo>=</mo><munderover><mo>&#8594;</mo><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>ii</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>(</mo><mi>iii</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>NaCN</mi><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>(</mo><mi>iv</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>dil</mi><mo>.</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>NaOH</mi></mrow></munderover><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><munder><mrow><mi>roduct</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>8</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></mrow></munder></math></p><p>The correct statement for product <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>B</mi></math> is. It is</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** optically active and adds one mole of bromine
- **(B)** racemic mixture and is neutral
- **(C)** racemic mixture and gives a gas with saturated <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NaHCO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> solution
- **(D)** optically active alcohol and is neutrall

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) racemic mixture and gives a gas with saturated <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NaHCO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> solution**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Propanal and methanal undergo aldol condensation reaction with sodium hydroxide. The <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#946;</mi></math>-unsaturated compound undergo <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo></math>conjugated addition with cyanide ion gives a racemic mixture. The cyanide undergo acidic hydrolysis gives carboxylic acid.</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/33d03f19-6a80-4647-93c7-50794c9da9ca-f0247494-7702-4af8-a6cd-674327490731-image.png" style="width: 615px; height: 465px;" /></p><p>Carboxylic acid will give <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> gas, with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NaHCO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> solution</p>

</details>

### Q34. JEE Main 2023 (29 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p>Find out the major products from the following reaction sequence.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/939bcca7-2171-4688-abe8-40d4042bf4b5-6b12e74a-062c-4fef-8b09-5b3d30f3c920-9895091.png" style="width: 588px; height: 157px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/1abf944d-4159-41e3-81d2-d31b53245c8d-9895091_A.png" style="width: 250px; height: 178px;" />
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d6e817e8-b084-405f-bb6d-4759db10604b-9895091_B.png" style="width: 250px; height: 198px;" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/4feccff3-c4fa-4e3d-a257-d0d33a4e7cb3-9895091_C.png" style="width: 250px; height: 185px;" />
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/815177cb-5aaa-4cb7-9040-c2c9c5b036bc-9895091_D.png" style="width: 300px; height: 272px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d6e817e8-b084-405f-bb6d-4759db10604b-9895091_B.png" style="width: 250px; height: 198px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

Cyanide ion acts as a nucleophile and attacks the carbonyl group of the methyl ketone forming a cyanohydrin ion, which on attack by ethanol ion leads to formation of carboxyl group and on further hydrolysis forms an ester. The alkanide ion from the grignard reagent then attacks the carbonyl of ester twice to form a diol with two alcohol groups attached to a <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msup></math> carbon. The sequence of reactions are given below:
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e142ce2a-7456-464f-a2d9-ac5b65ce6987-9895091_S.png" style="width: 500px; height: 587px;" />

</details>

### Q35. JEE Main 2023 (29 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

<p>Match List I with List II.</p><table border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1" style="width: 400px;">	<tbody>		<tr>			<td colspan="2" rowspan="1" style="text-align: center;">List I</td>			<td colspan="2" rowspan="1" style="text-align: center;">List II</td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td colspan="2" rowspan="1" style="text-align: center;">Reaction</td>			<td colspan="2" rowspan="1" style="text-align: center;">Reagents</td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>(A)</td>			<td>Hoffmann
			Degradation</td>			<td>(I)</td>			<td>Conc.<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>KOH</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>(B)</td>			<td>Clemmensen
			reduction</td>			<td>(II)</td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CHCl</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mi>NaOH</mi><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>(C)</td>			<td>Cannizzaro reaction</td>			<td>(III)</td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Br</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mi>NaOH</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>(D)</td>			<td>Reimer-Tiemann
			reaction</td>			<td>(IV)</td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Zn</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>Hg</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>HCl</mi></math></td>		</tr>	</tbody></table>

**Options:**

- **(A)** (A) - III, (B) - IV, (C) - II, (D) - I
- **(B)** (A) - II, (B) - IV, (C) - I, (D) - III
- **(C)** (A) - III, (B) - IV, (C) - I, (D) - II
- **(D)** (A) - II, (B) - I, (C) - III, (D) - IV

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) (A) - III, (B) - IV, (C) - I, (D) - II**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>(A) Hoffmann degradation reaction: Hoffmann developed a method for the preparation of primary amines by treating an amide with bromine in an aqueous or ethanolic solution of sodium hydroxide. </p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8741;</mo><mspace linebreak="newline"></mspace><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>Br</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi>NaOH</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>Na</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi>CO</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>NaBr</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math></p><p>(B) Clemmensen reduction reaction: The Clemmensen reduction is a reaction that is used to reduce aldehydes or ketones to alkanes using hydrochloric acid and zinc amalgam.</p><p>(C) Cannizzaro reaction: Aldehydes which do not have an <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi></math>-hydrogen atom, undergo self-oxidation and reduction (disproportionation) reaction on heating with concentrated alkali.</p><p>(D) Reimer-Tiemann reaction: On treating phenol with chloroform in the presence of sodium hydroxide, a &ndash;CHO group is introduced at ortho position of benzene ring. This reaction is known as Reimer-Tiemann reaction.</p><p>So, the correct matching is given in the following table:</p><table border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1" style="width: 350px;">	<tbody>		<tr>			<td style="text-align: center;">			<p>Reactions</p>			</td>			<td style="text-align: center;">Reagent used</td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>(A) Hoffmann degradation</td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Br</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>/</mo><mi>NaOH</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>(B) Clemmensen reduction</td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Zn</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>Hg</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>HCl</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>(C) Cannizzaro reaction</td>			<td>conc.KOH/ <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>KOH</mi><mo>/</mo><mo>&#8710;</mo></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>(D) Reimer-Tiemann reaction</td>			<td><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CHCl</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>,</mo><mi>NaOH</mi><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></math></td>		</tr>	</tbody></table>

</details>

### Q36. JEE Main 2023 (25 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>Number of compounds giving (i) red colouration with ceric ammonium nitrate and also (ii) positive iodoform test from the following is _________</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/4feb62c2-7211-45b1-8d21-d318f5f2c25d-9894611.png" style="width: 500px; height: 376px;" /></p><p> </p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Red colouration with ceric ammonium nitrate indicates presence of alcholic group, positive iodoform test indicates presence of a secondary alcohol which can oxidise to a methyl ketone. In total 3 compounds from the given will give positive test for both the reactions:
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/550697b7-7048-4c49-b91d-5d562bd229ef-9894611_2.png" style="width: 300px; height: 501px;" /></p><p> </p>

</details>

### Q37. JEE Main 2023 (24 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p>Compound <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mfenced></math> undergoes following sequence of reactions to give the Lactone <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></mfenced></math>.</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/abbd34ad-5ec5-4311-898a-9290e74278f3-9893508.PNG" style="width: 478px; height: 128px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e1716037-93dd-4fe7-af25-714197ea13e1-9893508_1.PNG" style="width: 169px; height: 150px;" />
- **(B)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e2f2a6b4-9b5e-40a6-804a-27538e6b66fa-9893508_2.PNG" style="width: 223px; height: 150px;" />
- **(C)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/5512e1c6-0b86-449b-a00f-edc4a80fe5b9-9893508_3.PNG" style="width: 360px; height: 94px;" />
- **(D)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/8ee6e327-d804-4d00-ae15-3441476984fd-9893508_4.PNG" style="width: 191px; height: 92px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e1716037-93dd-4fe7-af25-714197ea13e1-9893508_1.PNG" style="width: 169px; height: 150px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The cross aldol reaction takes place between isobutyraldehyde and formaldehyde to get hydroxypivaldehyde. On reaction with hydrogen cyanide followed by hydrolysis produces the lactone product.</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/048c981b-734c-466b-af6f-d4fa7f8efab3-9893508_5.PNG" style="width: 497px; height: 242px;" /></p>

### Q38. JEE Main 2023 (24 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>&#39;<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></math>&#39; formed in the following sequence of reaction is:</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/507fe933-6dc1-4be9-8d50-c5e5fcfdaa72-9893925.PNG" style="width: 455px; height: 109px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d3291e78-f0f2-49d9-a557-7d60aba9b9c4-9893925_!.PNG" style="width: 227px; height: 150px;" />
- **(B)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/0bf47ae7-6f61-4744-ba5b-c186e8532b55-9893925_2.PNG" style="width: 231px; height: 153px;" />
- **(C)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d6e65e46-e709-4016-8228-554ef9248552-9893925_3.PNG" style="width: 198px; height: 153px;" />
- **(D)** <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/68a46b24-2d7d-4b8d-baf1-5d969c10582a-9893925_4.PNG" style="width: 192px; height: 150px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/0bf47ae7-6f61-4744-ba5b-c186e8532b55-9893925_2.PNG" style="width: 231px; height: 153px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Ketonic part of the reactant reacts with sodium cyanide to give cyanohydrin and it cinverts to carboxylic acid on hydrolysis. The carboxylic acid part undergoes esterification reaction in the presence of ethyl alcohol in acidic medium. The ester part convert to tertiary alcohol with Grignard reagent.</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/3544765e-b911-44e8-a52a-2015ee80e570-9893925_5.PNG" style="width: 693px; height: 280px;" /></p>

</details>

### Q39. JEE Main 2022 (29 Jul Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

The number of stereoisomers formed in a reaction of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mo>&#177;</mo></mfenced><mi>PhC</mi><mfenced><mrow><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mfenced><mi>OH</mi></mfenced><mfenced><mi>CN</mi></mfenced><mi>Ph</mi></math> with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCN</mi></math> is

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **3**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Aldehydes and ketones undergo reaction with HCN to produce cyanohydrins. The reaction progresses very slowly by using pure hydrogen cyanide. Hence, base as a catalyst helps to speed up the reaction. This is an example of nucleophilic addition reaction.</p><p>here we are given racemic mixture of optically active compound. The attacking nucleophile can attack on carbonyl carbon from either side forming a new chiral carbon. So, in product there will be two chiral carbon but it is symmetrical. So, number of stereoisomers will be 3 only.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/935d4577-18df-4b04-9f7d-87cf4133bd34-image.png" style="width: 294px; height: 130px;" /></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q40. JEE Main 2022 (29 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p>Consider the following reaction sequence</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/bad7c4b6-4e76-4733-bd67-158ceaaaa9b9-image.png" style="width: 470px; height: 140px;" />​​​​​​​</p><p>The product &#39;<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math>&#39; is</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/f59531b4-2b76-4f78-88db-2a5d1da39482-image.png" style="width: 252px; height: 70px;" /></p>
- **(B)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/ac979fe6-4922-45b5-917f-ccecaa6baa7c-75a5f7d6-b036-4a97-9546-dd11225e947c-image.png" style="width: 280px; height: 68px;" /></p>
- **(C)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e5960d99-87b9-4dbc-bb1f-d9d4620d1704-image.png" style="width: 292px; height: 70px;" /></p>
- **(D)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/ba3b809c-dcb6-4702-8655-8c01de014fbc-image.png" style="width: 270px; height: 80px;" /></p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/ac979fe6-4922-45b5-917f-ccecaa6baa7c-75a5f7d6-b036-4a97-9546-dd11225e947c-image.png" style="width: 280px; height: 68px;" /></p>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Diisobutylaluminium hydride, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>AlH</mi><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>Bu</mi><msub><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msub></math>  also known as DIBAL or DIBAL-H, is an exceedingly useful and versatile reducing agent. It is an electrophilic reducing agent, usually employed in selective reductions of esters or nitriles to aldehydes</p><p>So, in first step nitrile group will be reduced to aldehyde group. </p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/c28ff58c-e447-4e6f-acf7-c45ba8d86919-image.png" style="width: 513px; height: 170px;" /></p><p>In second step cross aldol condensation reaction will take place which will give mixture of products. Out of which one of the product will be option B.</p></blockquote>

### Q41. JEE Main 2022 (26 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

<p>Which reactant will give the following alcohol on reaction with one mole of phenyl magnesium bromide <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi>PhMgBr</mi></mfenced></math> followed by acidic hydrolysis?</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/389c30db-f9c6-4b5d-b44a-33c36fa96b0b-image.png" style="width: 118px; height: 120px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>&#8211;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Ph</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#8801;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></math>
- **(C)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/137ea6dc-49e7-40a8-ba1a-cfc36d3ae774-image.png" style="width: 156px; height: 70px;" /></p>
- **(D)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/639a5f13-c409-43ad-a391-b2112b4ff454-image.png" style="width: 129px; height: 70px;" /></p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/639a5f13-c409-43ad-a391-b2112b4ff454-image.png" style="width: 129px; height: 70px;" /></p>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Phenyl magnesium bromide is called Grignard reagent. One mole of this react with carbonyl compound to give alcohols. It react with aldehyde to form secondary alcohol while with ketones gives tertiary alcohols. The given compound is a tertiary alcohol so, reactant should be a ketone.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/b9aa592f-226f-4376-b5d0-50fd57d5be83-image.png" style="width: 326px; height: 100px;" /></p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q43. JEE Main 2022 (25 Jul Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

<p>In the given reaction</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/53d8f725-3bbe-41fd-a644-f9aef03a9773-37c3a294-df6b-4681-9187-bab4b136d574-image.png" style="width: 332px; height: 147px;" /></p><p>(Where Et is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub></math>)</p><p>The number of chiral carbon/s in product <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is</p>

<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

The mechanism of the Claisen condensation reaction proceeds with the removal of an alpha proton through the action of a strong base to result in the formation of an enolate ion. </p><p>This results the formation products as follows,</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/03e6b3b6-d2c4-4655-8970-9e3e71778acb-image.png" style="width: 324px; height: 150px;" /></p></blockquote>

### Q44. JEE Main 2022 (29 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>In the given reaction,</p><p><img height="121" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/a32575e5-2463-45bd-90d2-d6df6edd6661-image.png" width="345" />    </p><p>The number of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></math> electrons present in the product <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#39;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>&#39;</mo></math> is ____________.</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

In an aldol condensation, an enolate ion reacts with a carbonyl compound in the presence of acid/base catalyst to form a &beta;-hydroxy aldehyde or &beta;-hydroxy ketone, followed by dehydration to give a conjugated enone. It is a useful carbon-carbon bond-forming reaction.<p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/b59c8e8e-e6b3-4da4-aee5-6f80f033991a-9711756_2.png" style="width: 550px; height: 185px;" /></p><p>As there are 2 pi bonds in the major product(P), the number of pi electrons will be equal to 4.</p>

</details>

The compound &#39;<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>&#39; is

- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/bf4940b9-5cd3-478f-91c8-56f017290290-image.png" style="width: 350px; height: 89px;" />

### Q46. JEE Main 2022 (28 Jun Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>The correct structure of product &#39;<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>&#39; formed in the following reaction,</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/ca2ce522-b33d-4466-998b-2b69ae7b4651-image.png" style="width: 480px; height: 83px;" /> </p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/fe741bd1-8e0b-4177-8e0a-58d4edd73de6-image.png" style="width: 140px; height: 109px;" />
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/8f108d57-86dd-44ce-88b8-d1a7f1c4a90e-image.png" style="width: 140px; height: 115px;" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/66c856b2-5a05-4b81-8ece-fea8e98ed311-image.png" style="width: 140px; height: 116px;" />

### Q47. JEE Main 2022 (26 Jun Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p>The final product &#39;<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>&#39; in the following reaction sequence</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/8c42aab0-de14-4538-9fb1-ccf95ac2de71-image.png" style="width: 368px; height: 70px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e6bb9369-fc21-4225-82e5-93085f60f9ba-image.png" style="width: 219px; height: 70px;" /></p>
- **(B)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/3fed946f-ef5e-4f5b-bd73-aaf77f9bd6c0-image.png" style="width: 181px; height: 70px;" /></p>
- **(C)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/6b5197ad-ff96-4475-a191-7c98323db88d-image.png" style="width: 219px; height: 110px;" /></p>
- **(D)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/2471b63e-5a3c-4131-8ac8-dab171e4cffd-image.png" style="width: 237px; height: 70px;" /></p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e6bb9369-fc21-4225-82e5-93085f60f9ba-image.png" style="width: 219px; height: 70px;" /></p>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Butan-2-one undergoes nucleophilic addition reaction with HCN to form a cyanohydrin which on hydrolysis forms 2-hydroxy-2methyl butanoic acid which undergoes dehydration to form 2-methyl but-2-enoic acid</p><p> </p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/8051029b-847e-4d96-8eac-1f87fb323c21-26-06-22nws.png" style="width: 400px; height: 186px;" /></p>

</details>

### Q48. JEE Main 2021 (01 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>Given below are two statements :</p><p>Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math> : The nucleophilic addition of sodium hydrogen sulphite to an aldehyde or a ketone involves proton transfer to form a stable ion.</p><p>Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>II</mi></math> : The nucleophilic addition of hydrogen cyanide to an aldehyde or a ketone yields amine as final product.</p><p>In the light of the above statements, choose the most appropriate answer from the options given below :</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** Both Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math> and Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>II</mi></math> are false.
- **(B)** Both Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math> and Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>II</mi></math> are true.
- **(C)** Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math> is false but Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>II</mi></math> is true.
- **(D)** Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math> is true but Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>II</mi></math> is false.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></math> is true but Statement <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>II</mi></math> is false.**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d1f9e204-a0c8-46f6-a503-afaa579a8659-a24b9a0d-7eb5-4846-931d-bf892d502885-image.png" style="width: 624px; height: 103px;" /></p><p>The nucleophilic addition of hydrogen cyanide to an aldehyde or a ketone yields cyanohydrin as final product.</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/66418797-6116-4653-a4e2-cb1a0e076cbd-887bec69-9630-4be3-84fb-7d77edc5f7a6-ss.png" style="width: 655px; height: 182px;" /></p>

</details>

### Q49. JEE Main 2021 (01 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

Experimentally reducing a functional group <strong>cannot</strong> be done by which one of the following reagents ?

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Zn</mi><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Pt</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Pd</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Na</mi><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Na</mi><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Sodium reacts with Hydrogen to form sodium hydride.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>Na</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8594;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>NaH</mi></math></p><p>So <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Na</mi><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> cannot be used for reduction of functional groups.</p><p>All other reagents are used as reducing agent.</p>

</details>

### Q50. JEE Main 2021 (31 Aug Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

The structures of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> formed in the following reaction are : <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="["><mrow><mi>Ph</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/79ca4416-bbfc-439f-ac8c-4698469925a1-image.png" style="width: 501px; height: 175px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/86948291-90ae-4d35-a10a-4a94722c5fac-image.png" style="width: 602px; height: 138px;" />
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/81952f26-e625-4310-8a50-e66763680c4a-image.png" style="width: 414px; height: 94px;" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/0b11d509-dbc4-41b9-b816-bbb13563762d-image.png" style="width: 608px; height: 131px;" />
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/12ef7d46-211f-4a03-9e7b-8610b7700f87-image.png" style="width: 418px; height: 91px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/86948291-90ae-4d35-a10a-4a94722c5fac-image.png" style="width: 602px; height: 138px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Friedel-Craft acylation reaction in benzene takes place. Aluminium chloride is Lewis acid which helps in generating electrophile as shown in first step of reaction.</p><p>This electrophile get substituted in benzene and product (A) is obtained. In next step, Clemmensen&#39;s reduction occur which converts aldehyde or ketone into hydrocarbon.</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/3aab6398-a5f3-45cb-a64b-7a0a6a310350-lambaaquestion.png" style="width: 600px; height: 263px;" /></p>

</details>

### Q51. JEE Main 2021 (31 Aug Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

For the reaction given below :
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/bac9703c-48d7-43e3-a913-f2f5f1041d8f-image.png" style="width: 366px; height: 151px;" />
The compound which is not formed as a product in the reaction is a :

**Options:**

- **(A)** diol
- **(B)** monocarboxylic acid
- **(C)** compound with both alcohol and acid functional groups
- **(D)** dicarboxylic acid

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) dicarboxylic acid**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Alpha hydrogen is not present in given carbonyl compound so, Cannizzaro reaction takes place. In this reaction one molecule get oxidised to salt of carboxylic acid and another molecule get reduced to alcohol. Hydrolysis of salt of carboxylic acid will produce carboxylic acid. Reaction can be represented as below:</p><p><img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/95be4e3b-5f2c-4b52-ba0d-00ebabc9eddf-bac9703c-48d7-43e3-a913-f2f5f1041d8f-image.png" style="width: 400px; height: 116px;" /></p><p>Clearly, dicarboxylic acid is not produced.</p>

</details>

### Q55. JEE Main 2021 (26 Aug Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

<p>The major products formed in the following reaction sequence <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> are:</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/a210bd34-6d4e-4a5d-a73d-fa958fb092c7-image.png" style="width: 336px; height: 131px;" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/4703437e-1763-485e-82fd-2466871841b7-image.png" style="width: 439px; height: 125px;" />
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/976e2ae7-e191-4215-9e1d-a776835b153e-image.png" style="width: 565px; height: 164px;" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/f33f0b89-5b69-4221-b482-25ef6672f6b3-image.png" style="width: 637px; height: 147px;" />
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e4c0cba4-7ce8-4bd4-a384-dfebacb498de-image.png" style="width: 514px; height: 116px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/4703437e-1763-485e-82fd-2466871841b7-image.png" style="width: 439px; height: 125px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>It is Haloform reaction. The haloform reaction is the reaction of a methyl ketone with chlorine, bromine, or iodine in the presence of hydroxide ions to give a carboxylate ion and a haloform. </p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/0652cbe8-eee0-4553-a8bb-7b965fdcd9a5-image.png" style="width: 650px; height: 157px;" /></p>

</details>

### Q57. JEE Main 2021 (18 Mar Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p><img height="123" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/341e3815-b8d6-4bfd-b9aa-d4227bc6f7ac-image.png" width="434" /></p><p>Consider the above reaction, the product <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></math> respectively are :</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/7937f907-4be4-4d38-bb0a-d699e5d931cc-image.png" style="width: 319px; height: 110px;" />
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/67cd5bb2-6cbd-4977-a4a6-76dc1cc6e9ea-image.png" style="width: 319px; height: 103px;" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/776bd5c2-7849-40cc-a727-d80caa724bc0-image.png" style="width: 319px; height: 100px;" />
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/9f8ef1be-93cd-4ff5-91c0-974ae05039bb-image.png" style="width: 304px; height: 102px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/776bd5c2-7849-40cc-a727-d80caa724bc0-image.png" style="width: 319px; height: 100px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>This is an aldol condensation reaction.There are few steps take place in this reaction:</p><p>Step 1: Acid base reaction takes place in between base and alpha hydrogen of cyclo pentanone and carbanion is formed.</p><p>Step 2: In this step formed carbanion behaves as nucleophile and attack on carbonyl compound carbon.</p><p>Step 3: Water gives proton and release hydroxide ion as shown below</p><p><img alt="" ### Q62. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/5ef0170a-c981-46f0-8b4c-3e469471b2db-image.png" style="width: 232px; height: 130px;" /></p><p>Which of the following reagent is suitable for the preparation of the product in the above reaction?</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** Red <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>Cl</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NaBH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>&#8861;</mo></mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#8853;</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Ni</mi><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>-</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>/</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>5</mn></msub><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mo>&#8861;</mo></mover><mover><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>&#8853;</mo></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>In the given reaction, only the carbonyl group has been reduced while the carbon- carbon double bonds are unaltered.</p><p>This is possible with the help of Wolf Kishner Reduction whose reagent set is given in Opiton (C).</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/f6045544-a78e-4433-8fe1-cb2d0e6f4657-YUII.png" style="width: 301px; height: 128px;" /></p><p>To reduce the carbonyl groups into alkane wolf - kischner reduction is used, without affecting the double bond.</p>

</details>

### Q65. JEE Main 2020 (03 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>The increasing order of the reactivity of the following compounds in nucleophile addition reaction is:</p><p>Propanal, Benzaldehyde, Propanone, Butanone</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** Benzaldehyde &gt; Butanone &lt; Propanone &lt; Propanal
- **(B)** Butanone &lt; Propanone &lt; Benzaldehyde &lt; Propanal
- **(C)** Propanal &lt; Propanone &lt; Butanone &lt; Benzaldehyde
- **(D)** Benzaldehyde &lt; Propanal &lt; Propanone &lt; Butanone

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) Butanone &lt; Propanone &lt; Benzaldehyde &lt; Propanal**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Rate</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>of</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>NAR</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>group</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>attache</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>to</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>carbonyl</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>carbon</mi></math>Moreover aldehydes are more reactive than ketones.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/01a515b0-7370-4aa6-b710-f75ba8708438-image.png" style="width: 436px; height: 117px;" /> </p>

</details>

### Q66. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p>Two compounds <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> with same molecular formula <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>6</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> undergo Grignard reaction with methylmagnesium bromide to give products <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math>. Products <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> show following chemical tests.</p><table border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1" style="width: 500px;">	<tbody>		<tr>			<td style="text-align: center;"><strong>Test</strong></td>			<td style="text-align: center;"><strong>C</strong></td>			<td style="text-align: center;"><strong>D</strong></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>Ceric ammonium nitrate Test</td>			<td>Positive</td>			<td>Positive</td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>Lucas Test</td>			<td>Turbidity obtained after five minutes</td>			<td>Turbidity obtained Immediately</td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td>Iodoform Test</td>			<td>Positive</td>			<td>Negative</td>		</tr>	</tbody></table><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> respectively are</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/727034c2-f87f-4240-aa35-c9ff30abc6c3-image.png" style="width: 400px; height: 104px;" />
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/6f8cc3c4-6435-4f84-be88-0b6fc6b2d9a8-image.png" style="width: 402px; height: 106px;" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/4159a267-278d-4f70-9400-a785c11403db-image.png" style="width: 440px; height: 97px;" />
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/a1c06898-3148-429a-90f4-0bf3acec4a53-image.png" style="width: 520px; height: 60px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/727034c2-f87f-4240-aa35-c9ff30abc6c3-image.png" style="width: 400px; height: 104px;" />**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>B will be ketone as it gives tertiary alcohol after reaction with grignard while A will be aldehyde which produces secondary alcohol after reaction with grignard reagent. </p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/050585e9-d9e0-4396-a754-1f4cc85df7f7-image.png" style="width: 525px; height: 390px;" /></p>

</details>

### Q67. JEE Main 2020 (09 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

Consider the following reactions <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>)</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>573</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow></mfrac><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">b</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">u</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi></math> . The mass percentage of carbon in <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> is _____________.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **66.67**

**Detailed Solution:**

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/a45e013e-8771-48d5-8b33-e03ea8b62c24.jpeg" /><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/71e42b20-f441-4ddd-aa3b-4c440509f6e9.jpeg" />
Compound A is <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/2f01d476-4186-42f9-a983-1ca4c60cb21f.jpeg" />
Percentage carbon in compound A= <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>12</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>48</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>16</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>×</mo><mn>100</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>66.67</mn></math>

</details>

### Q68. JEE Main 2020 (09 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Identify <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> in the following reaction sequence:
<img alt="" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/plmokn.PNG" style="width: 400px; height: 148px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/0b4b7627-f12a-43a9-a527-3f057203e42d.jpeg">
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d70dd8f2-3b0f-4cf7-aac4-92279248e806.jpeg">
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/76e61854-d5f1-42c9-9d0b-e906bb42777e.jpeg">
- **(D)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/35de9426-5c28-46c6-9d38-ff825799a06d.jpeg">

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d70dd8f2-3b0f-4cf7-aac4-92279248e806.jpeg">**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/3f64e7c4-4e57-4770-83f4-03090df6b33d.jpeg" /></p><p>Ozonolysis with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> converts alkenes into carbonyl compounds.</p><p>Alcohol upon reaction with protic acids tends to lose a molecule of water to form alkenes. These reactions are known as dehydrogenation or dehydration of alcohols. It is an example of an elimination reaction.The order of the ease of dehydration of alcohols is: tertiary &gt; secondary &gt; primary. 
 </p>
