### Q69. JEE Main 2020 (08 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The correct order of the calculated spin-only magnetic moments of complexes <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> to <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is:
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math><mi>N</mi><mi>i</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>O</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>N</mi><mi>i</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>O</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mi>C</mi><msub><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math><mi>N</mi><msub><mrow><mi>a</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>N</mi><mi>i</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>N</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math><mi>P</mi><mi>d</mi><mi>C</mi><msub><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>P</mi><msub><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>≈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>D</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&lt;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>B</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>d</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>P</mi><mi>P</mi><msub><mrow><mi>h</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>C</mi><msub><mrow><mi>l</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>P</mi><msup><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>N</mi><mi>i</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>O</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>N</mi><mi>i</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>S</mi><mi>F</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>N</mi><mi>i</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>C</mi><mi>N</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> <math><mi>N</mi><msup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>S</mi><mi>F</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>  
<math><msup><mrow><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>N</mi><mi>i</mi><msub><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mrow><mi>H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mi>O</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></msub></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup></math> <math><mi>N</mi><msup><mrow><mi>i</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>3</mn><msup><mrow><mi>d</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>W</mi><mi>F</mi><mi>L</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> <math><msubsup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mtext> </mtext><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mtext> </mtext><mn>2</mn></mrow></msubsup><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><msubsup><mrow><mi>e</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>g</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mtext> </mtext><mn>1</mn></mrow></msubsup></math> So, unpaired electron is <math><mn>2</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>M</mi><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>8</mn></msqrt><mi></mi><mi>B</mi><mi>M</mi></math>

</details>

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

**Options:**

- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></math> and <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/fff49d77-9b04-420a-b810-19b1f5da5c13-image.png" style="width: 200px; height: 125px;" />
- **(C)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/9dde26d8-79ce-47fe-8874-0653126e8f95-image.png" style="width: 150px; height: 87px;" />and <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/f1bcc9bf-4fa6-4c68-9098-156e20049dd5-image.png" style="width: 150px; height: 96px;" />
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math> and <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d451c673-b9e1-468c-9f19-71fb5e79bab7-image.png" style="width: 150px; height: 92px;" />

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Detailed Solution:**

Mechanism

### Q72. JEE Main 2019 (09 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

In the following reaction
carbonyl compound <math><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mover><mrow><mo>&#8652;</mo></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math>

Rate of the reaction is the highest for:

**Options:**

- **(A)** Acetone as substrate and methanol in stoichiometric amount
- **(B)** Propanal as substrate and methanol in excess
- **(C)** Propanal as substrate and methanol in stoichiometric amount
- **(D)** <p>Acetone as substrate and methanol in excess</p>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) Propanal as substrate and methanol in excess**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Acetal is form from the reaction of aldehyde with excess of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/9c894103-33d1-4e7d-97e5-851a80a590e4-image29.png" /></p><p>Formation of an acetal occurs when the hydroxyl group of a hemiacetal becomes protonated and is lost as water. The carbocation that is produced is then rapidly attacked by a molecule of alcohol. Loss of the proton from the attached alcohol gives the acetal.</p>

</details>

### Q74. JEE Main 2019 (12 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<p>The aldehydes which will not form Grignard product with one equivalent of Grignard reagents are
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>)</mo></math> <img height="126" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/62658b05-39bc-497f-8dc2-4cea45f21ee3-image.png" width="189" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>)</mo></math> <img height="140" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/d18b2e20-3e6e-414f-bcee-57f1baeb392b-image.png" width="285" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced></math> <img height="139" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/f8b4e728-d839-40dd-ba15-b04cc7de7c73-image.png" width="282" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mo>)</mo></math> <img height="141" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/6d601838-4369-4089-ade0-f52c50d6bef3-image.png" width="303" /></p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8201;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#8201;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>and</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> have active hydrogen (acidic hydrogen). So, acid base reaction will take place with Grignard reagent.

<img height="196" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/deb77259-8b50-41c9-839d-e51ec65efbf9-image.png" width="302" /></p><p><img height="196" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/1cac9c05-b2d4-4d38-822d-f91f140791cb-image.png" width="335" /></p>

</details>

### Q76. JEE Main 2019 (12 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

In the following reaction
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Aldehyde</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>Alcohol</mi><mover><mo>&#8594;</mo><mi>HCl</mi></mover><mi>Acetal</mi></math><table border="1" cellpadding="1" cellspacing="1" style="width:500px;">	<tbody>		<tr>			<td style="text-align: center;"><math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></math></td>			<td style="text-align: center;"><math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td style="text-align: center;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCHO</mi></math></td>			<td style="text-align: center;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>BuOH</mi></math></td>		</tr>		<tr>			<td style="text-align: center;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>CHO</mi></math></td>			<td style="text-align: center;"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>MeOH</mi></math></td>		</tr>	</tbody></table>The best combination is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCHO</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>MeOH</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>CHO</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>BuOH</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>CHO</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>MeOH</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCHO</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>t-</mtext><mi>BuOH</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>HCHO</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>MeOH</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>Formation of an acetal occurs when the hydroxyl group of a hemiacetal becomes protonated and is lost as water. The carbocation that is produced is then rapidly attacked by a molecule of alcohol. Loss of the proton from the attached alcohol gives the acetal.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e112117d-0477-4dbf-87ae-ea0358630ccf-image.png" style="width: 400px; height: 95px;" /></p></blockquote>

</details>

<p>In the following reactions, products <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> are:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <p><img height="220" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/2483b613-08e0-46ac-a291-66c40310abd2-image.png" width="337" /></p><p><img height="220" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/8cfed5ea-fdcd-409d-839c-fce99f947476-image.png" width="336" /></p>
- **(B)** <p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/95cfd0e8-14e7-4279-90ca-18a57b754a57-image.png" style="width: 459px; height: 156px;" /></p>
- **(C)** <p><img height="215" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/7a8196df-a5c6-4a45-b91e-ca4bc274f8c1-image.png" width="336" /></p><p><img height="215" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/174d423d-a729-4b75-8234-251e6ce7973a-image.png" width="337" /></p>
- **(D)** <p><img height="159" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/ed34ba5c-0a79-4803-bf3c-cc7c1d90d76d-image.png" width="463" /></p>

<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

### Q87. JEE Main 2017 (02 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

The correct sequence of reagents for the following conversion will be

<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/88582d34-6605-4d4f-8e77-4afcf41cca3b-image.png" style="width: 250px; height: 137px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>MgBr</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>/</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Ag</mi><msub><mfenced separators="|"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo></msup><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>MgBr</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Ag</mi><msub><mfenced separators="|"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo></msup><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup><mo>,&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>/</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Ag</mi><msub><mfenced separators="|"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo></msup><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>MgBr</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>/</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Ag</mi><msub><mfenced separators="|"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo></msup><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>/</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>MgBr</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mi>Ag</mi><msub><mfenced separators="|"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo></msup><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>/</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>MgBr</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The correct order of reagents for the following reaction is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>[</mo><mi>Ag</mi><msub><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msub><msup><mo>]</mo><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><msup><mi>OH</mi><mo>-</mo></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>/</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>OH</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>MgBr</mi></math> &amp; the reaction as well as mechanism is as follows with the correct order of reagents.</p><p>The reaction involves <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>SN</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> mechanism at second step which on further reaction with excess <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>CH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi>MgBr</mi></math> to give the final product.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/e18fe0e9-d54d-4d5d-94af-83e2cfcfaa19-image.png" style="width: 500px; height: 400px;" />

 </p>

</details>

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

In the reaction sequence

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mover accent="false"><mo>&#8594;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mover accent="false"><mo>&#8594;</mo><mrow><mo>&#8710;</mo></mrow></mover><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> ; the product B is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>
- **(B)** <img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/39b33271-3e67-490f-aa65-28b8dab68b7d-image.png" style="width: 160px; height: 73px;" />
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>This reaction is aldol condensation.</p><p><img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/79164ed3-657f-4956-9ca2-eab5c87f5b48-image.png" style="width: 550px; height: 151px;" /></p>

</details>

### Q89. JEE Main 2012 (Offline)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

In the given transformation, which of the following is the most appropriate reagent?
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/QbYIeXE0ieMpoHkLlkRhatE3H_ovt-YLOj8ITsmgezQ.original.fullsize.png">

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\mathrm{NH}_2 \mathrm{NH}_2, \stackrel{(-)}{\mathrm{OH}}$
- **(B)** $\mathrm{Zn}-\mathrm{Hg} / \mathrm{HCl}$
- **(C)** $\mathrm{Na}$, Liq. $\mathrm{NH}_3$
- **(D)** $\mathrm{NaBH}_4$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $\mathrm{NH}_2 \mathrm{NH}_2, \stackrel{(-)}{\mathrm{OH}}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\mathrm{ZnHg} / \mathrm{HCl}$ can't be used due to the presence of acid sensitive group i.e. $\mathrm{OH}$
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/G829JQPIFEsuve3PcLMSf1h86G7FHuPwPxCm-5FBNOI.original.fullsize.png">

and $\mathrm{Na} / \mathrm{Liq} . \mathrm{NH}_3$ and $\mathrm{NaBH}_4$ convert $-\mathrm{CO}-$ into $-\mathrm{CH}(\mathrm{OH})-$

</details>

### Q90. JEE Main 2011

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Trichloroacetaldehyde was subjected to Cannizzaro's reaction by using $\mathrm{NaOH}$. The mixture of the products contains sodium trichloroacetate and another compound. The other compound is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** Trichloromethanol
- **(B)** 2, 2, 2-Trichloropropanol
- **(C)** Chloroform
- **(D)** 2, 2, 2-Trichloroethanol

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 2, 2, 2-Trichloroethanol**

**Detailed Solution:**

$2 \mathrm{CCl}_3 \mathrm{CHO} \stackrel{\mathrm{OH}^{(-)}}{\longrightarrow} \mathrm{CCl}_3 \mathrm{COONa}+\mathrm{CCl}_3 \mathrm{CH}_2 \mathrm{OH}$
Cannizaro reaction is a disproportionation reaction
One aldehyde molecule is oxidized to salt of the carboxylic Acid, other one is reduced to Alcohol. So the compound is $\mathrm{CCl}_3 \mathrm{CH}_2 \mathrm{OH}$
IUPAC Name is $2,2,2,-$ Trichloro ethanol

</details>
